## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route from accelerated computing and the end of Moore's law, through AI, platform and software, to financials, per-market slides, a summary and the appendix, and its visual identity is consistent (green accent, grey panels, repeated chart styles). Slides 4, 12 and 13 are legible with clear hierarchy, and the market opportunity grid on slide 12 shows the $1T split quickly. The weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a dense wall of small legal text, many panels on slides 8, 11, 14 and 20 use very small text and low-contrast detail, and several titles are topic labels rather than takeaways. Slide 13 has overlapping labels in the operating profit chart, an unexplained shaded 1H bar, and donut charts with small, hard-to-compare slices.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent green and grey system, the bar-plus-margin-line financial charts, and the slide 12 market grid with large dollar figures.

**Most useful repair:** Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.

**What would change the score:** I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

_Confidence medium. 31 of 31 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a forward-looking statements and non-GAAP disclaimer, then a company overview stating NVIDIA's accelerated computing approach and scale. It moves through the case for accelerated computing, including an Amdahl's law example and the end of Moore's law, then covers AI demand, the Omniverse metaverse platform, the full-stack platform and developer flywheel, and the new software and services. Financial sections follow, showing revenue, operating profit, gross margin and cash flow from FY2018 to 1H FY23, alongside a breakdown by market platform: Data Center, Gaming, Professional Visualization and Automotive. Each platform gets its own slide with growth drivers. The deck closes with a summary and a $1 trillion market opportunity framing, followed by appendix reconciliations of non-GAAP to GAAP measures. Slides worth studying include the Amdahl's law illustration, the market opportunity slide with its segment sizing, and the financial charts that pair bars with margin lines.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the NVIDIA Investor Presentation from October 2022 have?**

The NVIDIA investor presentation has 31 pages. Slide 1 is the title slide, reading NVIDIA Investor Presentation, October 2022.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about Data Center revenue growth?**

Slide 17 shows Data Center revenue with a 66% 5-YR CAGR. The bar chart on that slide lists FY 2022 revenue at $10,613 and 1H FY 2023 revenue at $7,556, in millions.

**What market opportunity does the NVIDIA deck describe?**

Slide 12 is titled Giant Market Opportunity and labels it a $1 TRILLION OPPORTUNITY. The segment figures on that slide are $150B for NVIDIA AI Enterprise Software, $150B for Omniverse Enterprise Software, $100B for Gaming, $300B for Chips & Systems and $300B for Automotive.

**What example does the NVIDIA deck use to illustrate Amdahl's law?**

Slide 4 explains Amdahl's law and gives three examples. It states that if 90% of the runtime can be accelerated by 100x, the application is sped up 9x, and if 99% of the runtime can be accelerated by 100x, the application is sped up 50x.

### Slide 3

**Text**

NVIDIA pioneered accelerated computing to help solve impactful / challenges classical computers cannot.  A quarter of a century in the / making, NVIDIA accelerated computing is broadly recognized as the / way to advance computing as Moore’s law ends and AI lifts off.

NVIDIA’s platform is installed in several hundred million computers, / is available in every cloud and from every server maker, powers 357 / of the TOP500 supercomputers, and boasts over 3.5 million developers.

Headquarters: Santa Clara, CA  |  ~25,000 Employees

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a worked Amdahl's law example with concrete speed-up figures to make a technical argument visually plain.
- Review: Slides 4, 12 and 13 are legible with clear hierarchy, and the market opportunity grid on slide 12 shows the $1T split quickly.

**Text**

What Is Accelerated Computing?
A full-stack approach: silicon, systems, software
Not just a superfast chip – accelerated computing
is a full-stack combination of:
• Chip(s) with specialized processors
• Algorithms in acceleration libraries
• Domain experts to refactor applications
To speed-up compute-intensive parts of an application.
Amdahl’s law:
The overall system speed-up (S) gained by optimizing a
single part of a system by a factor (s) is limited by the
proportion of execution time of that part (p).
S = 1 over (1 − p) + p over s
For example:
• If 90% of the runtime can be accelerated by 100x,
the application is sped up 9x
• If 99% of the runtime can be accelerated by 100x,
the application is sped up 50x
• If 80% of the runtime can be accelerated by 500x,
or even 1000x, the application is sped up 5x

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left area (about 0 to 53% of width) holds the title, green subtitle, a thin grey horizontal rule at about 27.6% of height, the bullet text and a small diagram; a full-height light grey panel (x 52.9% to 100%) holds the centred Amdahl’s law block. Title is left-aligned at x 7.4%, top at 12.9%; body text shares the same 7.4% left margin, and the right-panel text is left-aligned at x 57% with the heading centred. NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at x 0.9%, y 94.8%; no footer text and no slide number are visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram (schematic illustration, not a chart or photo) below the bullet text, centred at about x 14.8% to 40%, y 65.6% to 90.3%: an upper row of 11 identical light grey rectangles (#D9D9D9) approximately 3.5% wide and 6% tall with small gaps, faint pale grey connector lines fanning from them down to three green (#76B900) filled circles about 1.8% wide. Below the circles, a light grey (#F2F2F2) rounded rectangle at x 20.6%, y 75.3%, w 13.7%, h 6.7% contains rows of small pale grey keys (about 6, 8 and 3 keys) like a keyboard-style chip block. Beneath it, a second light grey (#F2F2F2) rounded rectangle at x 20.6%, y 82.5%, w 13.7%, h 7.8% holds a row of 8 grey circles (#D9D9D9) with faint connector lines running down to three green circles at its bottom centre. No labels or arrowheads; the lines are thin and very light grey. The only other graphic is the NVIDIA logo in the bottom-left corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the left, with a flat light grey #F2F2F2 panel covering the right side from x 52.9% to 100%. Clean white and light grey corporate look with NVIDIA green accents. Title is bold black #000000 NVIDIASans-style grotesque sans-serif, about 4.45% of slide height. Subtitle is regular green #76B900 sans-serif, about 2.96%. Body text is regular black #000000 NVIDIASans-style sans-serif, about 2.72%. Bullet markers are green #76B900 Arial-style dots. Equations are set in Cambria Math, black #000000, about 3.7%. The horizontal rule is a thin mid-grey line. Right panel is #F2F2F2 with no border. No shapes have rounded corners on the panel, no shadows and no gradients; the diagram boxes are lightly rounded only.

### Slide 5

**Text**

Why Accelerated Computing?
Advancing computing in the post-Moore’s Law era
Accelerated computing is needed to tackle the most impactful opportunities of our time — like AI, climate simulation, drug discovery, ray tracing, and robotics.
NVIDIA is uniquely dedicated to accelerated computing – working top-to-bottom — refactoring applications and creating new algorithms, and bottom-to-top — inventing new specialized processors, like RT Core and Tensor Core.
“It’s the end of Moore’s Law as we know it.”
- John Hennessy Oct 23, 2018
“Moore’s Law is dead.”
- Jensen Huang, GTC 2013
Chart: Trillions of Operations per Second (TOPS); 10⁹ 10⁸ 10⁷ 10⁶ 10⁵ 10⁴ 10³ 10²; 1980 1990 2000 2010 2020 2030
GPU-Computing perf
2X per year
1000X
In 10 years
1.1X per year
1.5X perf per year
Single-threaded CPU perf

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: left text column from about x 7.4% to 47% holding the title block, a thin grey horizontal rule at about y 31%, and the body copy and quotations; right column from x 52.9% to 100% is a light grey panel (#F2F2F2) containing the full-height chart with its axes and labels. The title sits top-left at y 12.9%, left-aligned to the 7.4% margin. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at x 0.9%, y 94.8%. No slide number or other footer is visible.

**Visuals**

One line chart with a logarithmic vertical axis titled Trillions of Operations per Second (TOPS), ticks labelled 10^2 to 10^9 (decade spacing, about 56 px per decade on the slide image), and a horizontal linear time axis from 1980 to 2030 with ticks every 10 years, drawn as a thin grey baseline. Series 1, GPU-Computing perf, green #76B900: scatter markers with a solid line and dashed extension to about 2024; labelled ‘GPU-Computing perf 2X per year’. Markers, about: 2008 ≈ 6×10^4; 2009.6 ≈ 2×10^5; 2011 ≈ 5×10^5; 2012 ≈ 2×10^6; 2014 ≈ 1.6×10^7; 2016 ≈ 7×10^7; 2019 ≈ 2×10^8; 2022 ≈ 4×10^8; dashed extension to about 2024 near 10^9. Series 2, Single-threaded CPU perf, blue #0070C0: scattered dots from 1990 (about 15 TOPS) to about 2017 (about 1.4×10^4), solid trend line through the dots, then dashed flat extension to 2025 at about 1.5×10^4; labelled ‘1.1X per year’ near 2017 and ‘1.5X perf per year’ near 2000–2005 and ‘Single-threaded CPU perf’ below the start of the line. A vertical double-headed arrow in dark grey/black at about x 92% spans from about 10^8 to 10^5 and is labelled 1000X / In 10 years. No legend box; series are identified by direct labels. No points are highlighted beyond the arrow. Also a NVIDIA logo image at bottom-left.

### Slide 6

**Text**

AI Is the Greatest Technology Force of Our Time
Data centers across industries will become AI factories
AI has fundamentally changed what software can make and how you make software.
Companies are processing & refining their data, making AI software – becoming intelligence manufacturers. Their data centers are AI factories.
The first wave of AI learned perception and inference, like recognizing images, understanding speech, recommending a video, or an item to buy.
The next wave of AI is robotics – AI planning actions. Digital robots, avatars, and physical robots will perceive, plan and act.
NVIDIA’s acceleration stacks and ecosystems help bring AI to the world’s largest industries.
NVIDIA’s world-class AI expertise and scale can help revolutionize businesses.
Contact Center AI
500M Calls / Day
Meeting Transcription
3B Meeting Minutes / Day
Public Safety
>1B Smart City Cameras Deployed
Retail Asset Protection
$94.5B Inventory Loss / Year
Medical Imaging
10M Diagnostic Scans / Day
Industrial Inspection
32M Vision Sensors Installed by 2025
Transportation
10T Miles / Year
Credit Card Fraud
1.28B Credit Transactions / Day
Product Recommendations
1B E-Commerce Visitors / Day
Source: Nilson Report, IHS Markit, Similar Web, NRF, WHO, ABI and NVIDIA internal analysis

**Layout**

Centered title block across the top with the title on one line and a green subtitle beneath it, both centred on the slide. Below, a left-aligned text column occupies roughly the left 40 percent, and a 3 by 3 grid of image tiles with centred captions occupies the right 55 percent, starting at about 24 percent of slide height. A small source line sits bottom right of the grid at about 94 percent height, and the NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right with a green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Nine photographs in a 3 by 3 grid, each a sharp rectangular box with no rounded corners and a thin light grey edge, each with a dark #151617 rectangle at 61 percent opacity behind it that is hidden by the photo. Row 1: (1) Contact Center AI: a smiling woman with a headset in the foreground at left, with two men in headsets at computers behind her in a bright office, cool neutral tones, shallow depth of field. (2) Meeting Transcription: a 2 by 2 grid of video-call tiles showing four people: a man in a dark shirt at top left, a woman in a polka-dot blouse gesturing at top centre, a man with a headset at top right, a man at bottom left, a woman at bottom centre and a man in a dark suit at bottom right, with a bright window behind. (3) Public Safety: an aerial view of an old European town square with stone buildings and a clock tower, overlaid with colourful detection tags reading BUS, VAN, MOTORBIKE, PEDESTRIAN, CYCLIST and CAR in red, cyan, green, yellow and magenta boxes over the vehicles and people. Row 2: (4) Retail Asset Protection: a checkout counter with a blond male cashier at right and a customer, with a red circle drawn around a tray of meat and bananas, warm red and green tones. (5) Medical Imaging: a patient lying on a white MRI or CT table at left with a monitor at right showing a blue-tinted cross-section scan of a skull and brain, dark blue and white. (6) Industrial Inspection: a factory cell with yellow walls and blue robotic arms working on a blue and silver machine, bright overhead lighting. Row 3: (7) Transportation: a teal and white autonomous delivery pod with four black wheels on an urban street at dusk with a city skyline behind, cool grey and teal tones. (8) Credit Card Fraud: a hand holding a dark credit card over a keyboard, with a blue-lit screen showing a grid of round icons, dark blue and black tones. (9) Product Recommendations: a person viewed from behind holding a tablet showing an e-commerce site with shoe images and pink accents, warm beige background. Logo: NVIDIA logo (green eye mark with black NVIDIA wordmark) at bottom right, about 5.5 percent wide. A thin vertical green bar sits at the right edge near the bottom.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in NVIDIA Sans Bold (a geometric grotesque sans-serif), black #000000, about 4.45 percent of slide height. Subtitle in NVIDIA Sans Regular, green #76B900, about 2.96 percent. Body text in NVIDIA Sans Regular, black #000000, about 2.47 percent, with about 1.5 line spacing. Tile captions: titles in NVIDIA Sans Medium black about 1.98 percent, values in NVIDIA Sans Regular black about 1.48 percent. Source line in NVIDIA Sans Italic grey #8C8C8C about 1.23 percent. Accent green #76B900 used for subtitle, logo mark and right-edge bar. No gradients, shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 7

**Text**

Building and Operating Metaverse Applications Is the Next Wave
NVIDIA Omniverse — Runs on NVIDIA OVX servers | RTX workstations | Enterprise software | Cloud services
NVIDIA Omniverse is a software platform for
building and operating metaverse applications.
Our initial focus is on industrial metaverses,
such as digital twins used to emulate the
behavior of products or factories in the physical
world.
Omniverse uses a real-time, large-scale 3D
database that connects to 3D worlds via the
USD (Universal Scene Descriptor) framework.
Just as the internet connects websites over
HTML, Omniverse connects 3D worlds over USD.
Omniverse is essential for the next wave of
AI -- robotics -- where AI interacts with the
physical world.
Applications built to run on Omniverse are like
portals into the Omniverse virtual world.
Factory Digital Twin
Factory Planning
Factory CAD
Digital Human Training
Robot CAD
Robot Gym
Physics
AI
Path
Tracing
MDL
Product Development
Connecting 3D Creators &
AI Assistants in Virtual Worlds
Operate Digital Twin
Connecting Robots
in a Virtual World

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column (about x 7 to 39%) holds six body paragraphs top-aligned at about y 25%, and a right column (about x 42 to 93%) holds a single framed diagram image with a two-part caption beneath it. The title is centered at the top (y 7 to 12%) with a green centered subtitle line beneath it, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about x 93 to 99%, y 95 to 98%, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge. There is no slide number or footer text.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style digital illustration in a boxed rectangle at x 41.6%, y 24.1%, w 51.3%, h 51.5% (2732 by 1547 pixels), with a flat black background. Centre: a large circular, glowing, semi-transparent sphere (about x 52 to 75%, y 35 to 63%) containing a photograph of a factory assembly line with blue-uniformed workers and robotic arms, labelled 'Factory Digital Twin' above it. Satellite circles connected by glowing lines: top-left circle (about x 31 to 40%, y 29 to 42%) showing a grey isometric CAD factory floor, labelled 'Factory CAD'; top-right circle (about x 81 to 87%, y 27 to 38%) showing an aerial view of a factory floor, labelled 'Factory Planning'; middle-right circle (about x 84 to 92%, y 43 to 54%) showing a digital human walking on a grid, labelled 'Digital Human Training'; bottom-right circle (about x 81 to 87%, y 59 to 70%) showing a robot gym, labelled 'Robot Gym'; bottom-left circle (about x 42 to 51%, y 53 to 68%) showing a CAD robot cell, labelled 'Robot CAD'. Five blue circular badges with white 'USD' lettering sit on the connecting lines, at about x 56%/y 39%, x 80%/y 36%, x 55%/y 56%, x 80%/y 58%, and x 83%/y 49%. Three small round icons along the bottom centre of the sphere (about x 60 to 70%, y 62 to 72%) are labelled 'Path Tracing', 'AI' and 'MDL' (a sphere of coloured particles, a blue globe, and a group of figures) and a fourth round icon labelled 'Physics' (orange fire/particle sphere) sits to their right at about x 70%, y 63%. Lighting: dark background with warm white glow around the sphere and blue glow on connectors. Dominant colours: black, grey, white, blue (#0A5CD6 approx.) and warm amber. Not cropped; the image is a boxed rectangle with no border and no overlay. Below the image: a horizontal black arrow from about x 56% to 72% at y 79%, pointing right, joining 'Product Development' on the left and 'Operate Digital Twin' on the right. A small NVIDIA eye-and-wordmark logo in green and black at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white (#FFFFFF) field. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient, pattern or shadows. Title text is bold black (#000000) NVIDIA Sans Bold, sans-serif, about 4.45% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%). Subtitle is regular NVIDIA Sans in NVIDIA green #76B900, about 2.96% of slide height. Body text is regular NVIDIA Sans in black #000000 at about 2.47% of slide height with paragraph gaps of about 2.9% of slide height. Diagram labels are white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Regular/Medium at 1.1 to 1.5% of slide height on the dark image. Caption headings are medium black at 2.2% and sub-captions regular black at 1.7%. Accent: NVIDIA green #76B900 for subtitle, logo and right edge bar. Shape styles: the arrow is a 1 px black line with a small filled arrowhead; the diagram box has square corners and no border.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a dense wall of small legal text, many panels on slides 8, 11, 14 and 20 use very small text and low-contrast detail, and several titles are topic labels rather than takeaways.
- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.

With nearly three decades of a singular
focus, NVIDIA is expert at accelerating
software and scaling compute by a
Million-X, going well beyond Moore’s law.
Accelerated computing is a full-stack
challenge, demanding deep understanding
of the problem domain, optimizing across
every layer of computing, and all three chips
– GPU, CPU, and DPU.
Scaling across multi-GPUs and multi-nodes
is a datacenter-scale challenge and requires
treating the network and storage as part of
the computing fabric.
Our platform extends from PCs to
supercomputing centers, enterprise data
centers, cloud and edge environments.

**Layout**

Centred two-line title block at the top with the title above a green subtitle. Below, a left-hand diagram occupies roughly x 5 to 65 percent: a row of 14 framework thumbnails with a label above, then a PLATFORMS row of three green circles, then two grey rounded panels with right-aligned row labels. The right column of body text starts at x 68.2 percent, top-aligned at y 26.7 percent, with left-aligned ragged-right lines and paragraph gaps. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a green bar at the far right edge; no slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram of an accelerated computing stack with four tiers. Tier 1, AI APPLICATION FRAMEWORK: 14 square thumbnail images in one row, each labelled in white uppercase on a dark band: MODULUS (colourful heat-map style plot on dark background), MONAI (green organ-like shape on dark), RIVA (figure in a lab setting), MAXINE (person in a video frame), NEMO (blue water-drop character), MERLIN (white robotic-looking devices on a grey table), CUOPT (small grey autonomous vehicle on a street), MORPHEUS (dark field of orange dots), TOKKIO (purple-haired digital human on dark background), AVATAR (digital human face), DRIVE (street scene with cars), ISAAC (white robotic arm), METROPOLIS (dark city map with red and teal markers), HOLOSCAN (orange and blue particle cloud). Thin light-grey lines run from the framework tiles down to the platform circles. Tier 2, PLATFORMS: three solid green circles (#76B900) each about 7 percent of slide height, with white line icons: a three-orbit atom symbol (NVIDIA HPC) at x 20.4, a network-node cluster (NVIDIA AI) at x 33.1, and a concentric swirl (NVIDIA Omniverse) at x 45.8, each followed by its black label. Tier 3, ACCELERATION LIBRARIES: a light-grey rounded panel containing three rows of grey pill-shaped labels: row one cuNumeric, CV-CUDA, cuQuantum, Parabricks, Sionna, Jetpack; row two RAPIDS, Spark, cuDNN, cuGraph, TensorRT, Triton, Deepstream, Flare; row three DOCA, Mag IO, Aerial (centred). Tier 4, CLOUD-TO-EDGE / DATACENTER-TO-ROBOTIC SYSTEMS / 3-CHIPS: a second light-grey rounded panel with eight grey-outlined circles in one row: RTX, DGX, HGX, EGX, OVX, Super POD, AGX, IGX; faint grey connector lines fan from these circles down to three green chip icons below, labelled GPU (grid-of-cells chip icon), CPU (four-square chip icon) and DPU (dense grid chip icon). Connectors are thin light grey lines running from each upper circle to the chips. No photographs other than the 14 framework thumbnails; the NVIDIA wordmark logo bottom right in green and black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with a minimal technical diagram style. Title in NVIDIASans Bold, black #000000, about 4.5 percent of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in NVIDIASans Regular, green #76B900, about 3.0 percent of slide height. Body text in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 2.5 percent of slide height, with generous line spacing. Row labels in NVIDIASans Medium caps, black #000000, about 1.7 percent of slide height. Pill and circle labels in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 1.2 percent of slide height. Panels filled #F2F2F2 with a thin #D0D0D0 outline and rounded corners of about 1.5 percent of slide width; pills filled #DCDCDC with a #BDBDBD outline and rounded ends; chip-tier circles filled #F2F2F2 with a #BDBDBD outline; platform and chip icons filled #76B900 with white line glyphs. Thumbnails are square with sharp corners and no border. Accent green bar at the right edge, #76B900. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 9

**Text**

NVIDIA’s Multi-Sided Platform and Flywheel
NVIDIA is valued by every stakeholder in the ecosystem:
• For developers – NVIDIA’s One Architecture and large
installed base give developer’s software the best
performance and greatest reach
• For computer makers and CSPs – NVIDIA’s rich suite
of Acceleration Platforms lets partners build one
offering to address large markets including media
& entertainment, healthcare, transportation, energy,
financial services, manufacturing, retail, and more
• For customers – NVIDIA is offered by virtually every
computing provider and accelerates the most
impactful applications from cloud to edge
• For NVIDIA – Deep engagement with developers,
computing providers, and customers in diverse
industries enables unmatched expertise, scale,
and speed of innovation across the entire accelerated
computing stack – propelling the flywheel

**Layout**

Centred bold title across the top at about 7 to 12 percent of slide height. Below it two thin grey bordered rectangular panels sit side by side: a left text panel spanning about 4.5 to 49 percent of width and a right diagram panel spanning about 51 to 95.5 percent, both from about 24 to 91 percent of height. Left-aligned body text sits inside the left panel with a bulleted list. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about 93 to 98.6 percent width, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Right panel: a flywheel diagram of four green rounded rectangles arranged in a diamond, with curved black arrows running clockwise between them. Top box (about 67 to 79 percent x, 27 to 41 percent y) reads 3.5 Million Developers. Right box (about 80 to 92 percent x, 50 to 64 percent y) reads Global Computer Makers and CSPs. Bottom box (about 67 to 79 percent x, 73 to 87 percent y) reads 35,000 Organizations Across Industries. Left box (about 54 to 66 percent x, 50 to 64 percent y) reads NVIDIA Accelerated Computing. Arrow 1 runs from the top box down-right to the right box, labelled Wealth of Accelerated Apps on its right. Arrow 2 runs from the right box down-left to the bottom box, labelled 100’s of Systems CSPs Worldwide and Full-Stack Acceleration for Largest Industries below. Arrow 3 runs from the bottom box up-left to the left box, labelled Demand and New Opportunities on its left. Arrow 4 runs from the left box up-right to the top box, labelled Full-Stack Expertise and Scale and CUDA Installed Base on its left. Arrowheads are small and open. Bottom right: NVIDIA logo, a green eye-mark with black NVIDIA wordmark, at about 93 to 98.6 percent x, 94.8 to 98 percent y. The green bar is a solid vertical rectangle at the right edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in NVIDIA Sans Bold, black #000000, about 4.45 percent of slide height, centred. Body text in NVIDIA Sans Regular, black #000000, about 2.72 percent of slide height, with bold lead-ins for each bullet. Bullet markers are green #76B900 Arial. Flywheel boxes are solid green #76B900 with white #FFFFFF NVIDIA Sans Regular text at about 2.22 percent of slide height; boxes have rounded corners of roughly 10 percent of box height and no shadow. Diagram labels are black #000000 NVIDIA Sans Regular at about 1.98 percent. Panel borders are thin 1 pt mid-grey #999999 rectangles with square corners and no fill. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Full-Stack & Data Center Scale Acceleration
Drive Significant Cost Savings and Workload Scaling
Classical Computing – 92 CPU-only servers
$3.3M (including, switches, cables, racks)
Application
CPU server racks
Accelerated Computing – 1 NVIDIA DGX A100
$220,000 DGX and $100,000 NVIDIA AI software
Application
Re-Engineered for Acceleration
CUDA-X Acceleration Libraries
Magnum IO
DGX
10X lower cost
14X better energy-efficiency
Cost comparison example based on latest available NVIDIA A100 GPU and Intel CPU inference results in the commercially available category of
the MLPerf industry benchmark; includes related infrastructure costs such as networking.

**Layout**

Centred title block across the top, with the title at about 7 percent from the top and the green subtitle beneath it. Two equal bordered panels sit side by side in the lower two-thirds, the left panel from about x 4.5 to 49 and the right from about x 51 to 95.5, both spanning y 24 to 91. Each panel has a centred heading, a centred subline, and a vertical stack of boxes. A small italic source note sits bottom-left under the panels at x 4.5, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right at about x 93, y 95, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Two diagram panels. Left panel: a stack of a green rounded box labelled Application, a light grey rounded box labelled CPU server racks containing five identical server-rack icons in a row (each a stack of six white rectangles outlined in green with small green dash markers). Right panel: a stack of three green rounded boxes labelled Application over Re-Engineered for Acceleration, CUDA-X Acceleration Libraries and Magnum IO, followed by a green outlined icon of a DGX server box with the label DGX to its right. Below the right stack are the bold lines 10X lower cost and 14X better energy-efficiency. Icons are flat line drawings in NVIDIA green #76B900 with white fill. The NVIDIA logo is the green and black wordmark in the bottom right corner. The slide has no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a dense wall of small legal text, many panels on slides 8, 11, 14 and 20 use very small text and low-contrast detail, and several titles are topic labels rather than takeaways.
- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.

**Text**

New NVIDIA Software and Services / Enabling the World’s Enterprises to Revolutionize Industries with AI / NVIDIA AI Enterprise / The operating engine of AI for / end-to-end data-driven software / development. / One engine license accelerates end- / to-end modern AI and data science. / One engine license unlocks wealth / of data processing, AI, and robotics / frameworks and applications – / e.g., RAPIDS, Spark, Merlin, Monai, / Metropolis, cuOpt, Morpheus, Tokkio. / Per GPU On-Prem Subscription / Per GPU-HR Cloud Consumption / NVIDIA Omniverse / A platform for designing, building, / and operating 3D and virtual world / simulations. / Consists of a virtual world engine, / USD connectors, and portals browsing / the virtual world simulation. / Omniverse is an enterprise application / that connects architects, designers, / hardware and software engineers, / marketers, to supply-chain and / factory planners. / Per Connection On-Prem Subscription / Per GPU-HR Cloud Consumption / NVIDIA Nemo LLM / NVIDIA-hosted cloud service for / training Large Language Models / to perform specific tasks – / e.g., summarize legal documents, / write marketing copy, analyze market / sentiment, chatbot to support / customers, search documents, write / and document code, paraphrase / Nemo can help thousands of / companies, train language AI’s to / do hundreds of tasks, in 10’s of / languages. / Per GPU On-Prem Subscription / Per GPU-HR Cloud Consumption / NVIDIA BioNemo / NVIDIA-hosted cloud service for / training and deploying large / biomolecular models that / understand the language of / chemistry, proteins, RNA, and DNA. / BioNemo can help researchers, / biotech, and pharma companies / to process chemical and biological / datasets to accelerate drug / discovery. / Per GPU On-Prem Subscription / Per GPU-HR Cloud Consumption

**Layout**

Four equal-width columns of bordered cards sit in a row below a centred title and green subtitle, all centred on the slide. Each card has a centred heading, a centred body block and a grey footer band at the bottom of the card, with about 4.5% side margins; the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right and a short green bar sits at the far right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Four rectangular cards, each a thin grey outline box with a light grey footer band, and a small NVIDIA logo at bottom right. No charts, photographs or illustrations. The footer bands are measured as four equal grey rectangles (#F2F2F2 with a thin grey #A6A6A6 border) at the bottom of each card, and the cards themselves are white boxes with thin grey #A6A6A6 outlines.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black titles and body text, green #76B900 subtitle and accent bar. Sans-serif typeface NVIDIASans (close to Helvetica-style grotesque): title bold, card headings medium, body regular. Title is about 4.45% of slide height (about 6.5% cap height); body text about 1.98% of slide height. Thin grey outlines, square corners, no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Centred title sits at the top of the slide, roughly 7% from the top edge. A row of seven evenly spaced icon-and-label columns runs across the upper third, centred on the slide width. Below, a left text column (about x 7% to 41%) holds five centred green-headed text blocks stacked vertically, and a right block (about x 44% to 93%) holds a full-width green banner above a grid of grey boxes: a tall GAMING column, two equal boxes side by side above a wide CHIPS & SYSTEMS box, and a tall AUTOMOTIVE column. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right at about x 93%, y 95%; there is no visible slide number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Seven single-colour line icons in NVIDIA green #76B900 with labels above each. (1) Gaming & Metaverse: game controller outline with plus sign, two circular buttons and a small button cluster on the right. (2) Financial Services: three candlestick-chart glyphs with rectangular bodies and vertical wicks, of varying heights. (3) Healthcare: a boxed rack-like server or medical-scanner glyph with horizontal lines and a small round element at the bottom. (4) Logistics: delivery truck outline with a box body, cab and two wheels. (5) Manufacturing: stacked boxes on a shelf beside a small cart carrying a box. (6) Retail: a sock, shorts and a T-shirt with two bent arrows connecting them. (7) Transportation: side-view car outline with two wheels and three curved signal arcs above the roof. Icons are about 6.6% wide and 11.8% tall, line weight thin and uniform. Right-hand panel: a solid green banner #76B900 with white uppercase text; below, grey rectangles #EBEBEB with thin light grey #C8C8C8 borders and no shadows or rounding. Values printed: Gaming $100B; NVIDIA AI Enterprise Software $150B; Omniverse Enterprise Software $150B; Chips & Systems $300B; Automotive $300B. No chart, table or photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title is black bold NVIDIA Sans-style sans-serif, large, about 4.5% of slide height. Labels and box headings are black medium-weight sans-serif around 2% of height. Green headings are bold NVIDIA-style sans-serif #76B900. Body text is regular black sans-serif #000000 around 2% of height. Dollar figures are green #76B900 medium sans-serif at about 4.1% of height (the bottom-right Chips & Systems figure about 4.4%). Accents are NVIDIA green #76B900 throughout; a thin vertical green bar sits at the far right edge near the bottom. Grey boxes #EBEBEB have square corners and no shadow.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Pairs revenue bars with an operating margin line for the same fiscal years, letting the reader see growth and profitability together.
- Review: Slides 4, 12 and 13 are legible with clear hierarchy, and the market opportunity grid on slide 12 shows the $1T split quickly.
- Review: Slide 13 has overlapping labels in the operating profit chart, an unexplained shaded 1H bar, and donut charts with small, hard-to-compare slices.
- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.

**Layout**

Three panels on a white field: a tall revenue column chart boxed on the left (about x 9.5 to 49, y 27 to 92), and on the right a top operating profit combo chart box (about x 50.8 to 95.5, y 23.6 to 50) above a lower box holding two donut charts (about x 50.8 to 95.5, y 52.9 to 82.2). The title is centred across the top at y 7.3. Footnotes sit in grey italic under the right boxes. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right and a thin green bar sits at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, column chart titled Revenue ($M), value axis not printed, categories FY 2018 to FY 2023 on a baseline. Values: FY 2018 $9,714; FY 2019 $11,716; FY 2020 $10,918; FY 2021 $16,675; FY 2022 $26,914; FY 2023 $14,992. Bars are two-tone stacks in #76B900 with darker #598B00 segments labelled 1H for FY 2022 and FY 2023; a translucent pale green #76B900 at 30 percent opacity bar sits between FY 2022 and FY 2023 with a black arrow from the FY 2022 1H segment top to the FY 2023 bar. Chart 2, combo chart with bars labelled Operating Profit (Non-GAAP) in #76B900 and a black line labelled Operating Margin (Non-GAAP). Bar values: FY 2018 $3,617 (margin 37%); FY 2019 $4,407 (38%); FY 2020 $3,735 (34%); FY 2021 $6,803 (41%); FY 2022 $12,690 (47%); FY 2023 $5,280 (35%). FY 2022 and FY 2023 bars have darker #598B00 1H segments. Margin line in black about 1 pt; no separate value axis printed; margin values in white inside bars. Chart 3, two donut charts with no axes. 1H FY18 donut: Gaming 53 in #76B900, Data Center 20 in #008564, ProViz 10 in #6A1B9A-like purple, Auto 7 in #0076C0-like blue, OEM & Other 10 in #8C0A5A-like magenta, segments clockwise from top. 1H FY23 donut: Gaming 38 in #76B900, Data Center 51 in #008564, ProViz 7 in purple, Auto 2 in blue, OEM & Other 2 in magenta, with a legend to the right listing Gaming, Data Center, ProViz, Auto, OEM & Other in their series colours. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with eye symbol in #76B900 and black text, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black titles and body text. Accent colours are NVIDIA green #76B900, dark green #598B00, teal #008564, purple, blue and magenta in the donuts. Title is bold NVIDIA Sans style sans-serif, about 4.45 percent of slide height. Chart labels are NVIDIA Sans Regular about 1.2 to 1.7 percent of slide height. Captions are NVIDIA Sans Italic in grey #8C8C8C. Boxes have thin grey #8C8C8C outlines and square corners with no shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a dense wall of small legal text, many panels on slides 8, 11, 14 and 20 use very small text and low-contrast detail, and several titles are topic labels rather than takeaways.
- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.

**Text**

NVIDIA Gross Margins Reflect Value of Acceleration / Accelerated computing requires full-stack and data / center-scale innovation across silicon, systems, / algorithms and applications. / Significant expertise and effort are required, but / application speed-ups can be incredible, resulting in / dramatic cost and time-to-solution savings. / For example, 10 NVIDIA HGX nodes with 80 NVIDIA A100 / GPUs that cost $4M can replace 920 nodes of CPU servers / that cost over $50M for AI inference. / NVIDIA chips carry the value of the full-stack, not just / the chip. / Gross Profit (Non-GAAP) / Gross Margin (Non-GAAP) / 60% / $5,844 / 62% / $7,233 / 63% / $6,821 / 66% / $10,947 / $17,969 / 67% / 1H / $8,636 / 1H / 58% / FY 2018 / FY 2019 / FY 2020 / FY 2021 / FY 2022 / FY 2023 / Cost comparison example based on latest available NVIDIA A100 GPU and Intel CPU inference results in the commercially available category of / the MLPerf industry benchmark; includes related infrastructure costs such as networking. / 1H FY23 financial metrics reflect a Gaming channel inventory correction and a $1.2B charge for inventory and related reserves. / Fiscal year ends in January. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures. Gross margins are rounded to the nearest percent.

**Layout**

Two-column content layout on a white slide: a bordered text panel on the left (about x 4.5 to 49.1 percent, y 23.6 to 81.9 percent) and a bordered chart panel on the right (about x 50.9 to 95.4 percent, same height). The title is centred across the top at y about 7.3 percent with no subtitle, and the panels sit below a wide empty band. Left and right margins are about 4.5 percent; the footnotes sit under each panel at y about 83 percent, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at y about 94.8 percent with a green vertical bar at the far right edge. The slide has no visible slide number.

**Visuals**

One combination chart in the right panel. Type: stacked column chart with a line overlay on the same panel. Title: none printed as a chart title; the legend reads Gross Profit (Non-GAAP) and Gross Margin (Non-GAAP). Axes: no visible value axis or tick labels (the file contains white-on-white tick text $0, $6,000, $12,000 and $18,000 at the left and 55% to 69% at the right, which is not visible on the rendered slide); category axis shows FY 2018 to FY 2023 along a thin black baseline. Series 1, Gross Profit (Non-GAAP), green columns #76B900 with labelled values: FY 2018 $5,844; FY 2019 $7,233; FY 2020 $6,821; FY 2021 $10,947; FY 2022 $17,969 (top segment #76B900 with 67% printed in white and a lower segment #598B00 labelled 1H); FY 2023 $8,636 (fully #598B00 with 1H and 58% in white). Series 2, Gross Margin (Non-GAAP), thin black line with labelled points: 60% at FY 2018, 62% at FY 2019, 63% at FY 2020, 66% at FY 2021, 67% at FY 2022, and the line ends at FY 2023 at about 58% with no printed label outside the bar. Legend: small green square #76B900 before Gross Profit (Non-GAAP) and a short black dash before Gross Margin (Non-GAAP), at the top of the panel. Highlighted points: the FY 2022 column is split into two tones at about the midpoint, and FY 2023 is the darker shade throughout. No photographs, icons or logos other than the NVIDIA logo bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Background white #FFFFFF. Title in NVIDIASans Bold, black #000000, sentence case, single line, approximately 4.5 percent of slide height. Body text in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 2.7 percent of slide height for the main paragraphs and about 2.5 percent for the indented example. Chart labels in NVIDIASans Regular about 1.7 percent, with white in-bar labels. Footnotes in NVIDIASans Italic, grey #8C8C8C, about 1.2 percent. Accents: bright green #76B900 for light columns and legend key, dark green #598B00 for the 1H segment and FY 2023 column, black #000000 for the margin line. Panel outlines are thin 1 px mid-grey #8C8C8C rectangles with square corners, no fill and no shadow. No gradients or shadows anywhere.

**Layout**

Single-slide layout with a centred bold title at the top spanning the middle third, above two side-by-side outlined panels of equal size (left panel for the bar chart, right panel for Capital Allocation) occupying roughly the middle band of the slide. Both panels have headings centred at the top of each box. A small italic footnote sits left-aligned below the left panel, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a thin green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled Free Cash Flow (Non-GAAP), with no printed y-axis or gridlines, a horizontal baseline, and six categories along the x-axis labelled FY 2018, FY 2019, FY 2020, FY 2021, FY 2022 and FY 2023. Each bar has its value printed above it: FY 2018 $2.9B, FY 2019 $3.1B, FY 2020 $4.3B, FY 2021 $4.7B, FY 2022 $8.0B, FY 2023 $2.2B. The bars are light green #76B900 for full-year values. FY 2022 is a stacked bar: its lower segment is dark green #598B00 and labelled 1H, and its upper segment is light green. FY 2023 is dark green #598B00 and labelled 1H, indicating a first-half figure. Bars are drawn with flat fills and no outlines. Approximate bar heights are proportional to the printed values, with no axis units shown. Two thin grey divider lines with fading ends separate the Capital Allocation sections. The NVIDIA logo (green and black wordmark) sits at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles and body text black #000000. Section headings and accents bright green #76B900. Dark green #598B00 for the 1H bar segments. Footnote in mid grey #8C8C8C. Typeface is NVIDIASans, a geometric sans-serif: Bold for the main title, Medium for panel and section headings, Regular for body and chart labels, Italic for the footnote. Main title about 4.45% of slide height, panel headings about 2.47%, body and labels about 2.2% and 1.7%, footnote about 1.2%. Panels have thin grey #999999 1px outlines with square corners and no fill, no shadows and no gradients apart from the fading divider lines.

**Layout**

Four equal-width columns of photo-over-text cards sit in one row across the middle of the slide, with a generous empty band above (between the title and the photos) and below the body text. The slide title is centred at the top, above the columns. Each column is centred: photo, green category heading, grey percentage line, thin divider, revenue and CAGR block, then product list. The NVIDIA logo sits at the bottom right with a green vertical accent bar at the far right edge. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Four rectangular photographs, one per column, all the same size (about 21.8% by 36.0% of the slide), set in a horizontal row with equal gaps. Column 1 (Gaming): a blocky, Minecraft-style voxel cave scene with brown and grey stone walls, green vines hanging down, light shafts entering from the upper right, and a wooden railing and planks in the foreground; warm, muted brown and green tones; full frame, not cropped to a shape, square corners. Column 2 (Data Center): a dark server rack seen head-on, with three large beige-grey GPU/system modules on the left, black chassis, rows of small blue-lit networking units in the centre and a small NVIDIA logo on the module edges; black and beige palette with blue accent lights; full frame. Column 3 (Professional Visualization): an architectural interior render with a curved pale ceiling dome at top, a glass wall with dark wooden mullions on the right, sunlight streaking across a cream wall on the left, a red accent panel at upper left, and grey chairs, small tables and potted plants on a wooden floor; warm beige, cream and blue-grey tones; full frame. Column 4 (Automotive): a silver-grey concept sedan viewed front three-quarter, with a illuminated blue LED grille bearing a Mercedes-Benz star, blue headlight strips, and five-spoke dark wheel; behind it a glass-walled building, green trees, a white fire hydrant on the left and a grey paved road; silver, green and blue tones; full frame. Each photo has a dark #151617 rectangle directly behind it at 61% opacity, which is hidden by the opaque photo. Four thin grey gradient divider lines (fading at both ends) sit under the category headings. The NVIDIA logo (green and black wordmark) is at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decorative graphics beyond the photos and a green accent bar. Titles and category headings: title is black #000000 bold sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, geometric grotesque) at about 4.45% of slide height; category headings are green #76B900 medium-weight sans-serif at about 2.47%. Body text is black #000000 regular-weight sans-serif at about 1.98% of slide height; percentage lines are regular at about 1.73%; revenue lines are bold at about 1.98%. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900. Divider lines are thin grey gradients, roughly 0.2% of slide height thick. Photos have square corners, no shadows, no borders. Text is set in centred alignment throughout.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Gives each platform a consistent layout of headline claims, a revenue chart with CAGR and bulleted growth drivers.
- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Layout**

Single centred title block at the top with the title 'Data Center' above a green subtitle, followed by two side-by-side white boxed panels of equal width: a bar chart on the left (about 4.5% to 49.2% of slide width) and a centred text panel on the right (about 50.9% to 95.5%), both spanning roughly 23.6% to 82.0% of slide height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about 93% to 99% width, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge; no slide number or footer text is shown.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Revenue ($M)' with a subtitle '66% 5-YR CAGR' at upper left of the plot. Vertical bars for six fiscal years, left to right: FY 2018 $1,932; FY 2019 $2,932; FY 2020 $2,983; FY 2021 $6,696; FY 2022 $10,613; FY 2023 $7,556 (values printed above each bar; no axis, axis labels or gridlines are printed). Bars FY 2018 to FY 2021 are solid bright green #76B900 with no visible stacking. FY 2022 is a tall bright green bar with its bottom segment (about 0 to 17 percent of its height) dark olive #598B00 labelled '1H' in white. FY 2023 is a dark olive #598B00 bar labelled '1H' in white at its upper segment. Between the FY 2022 and FY 2023 bars a light green (#76B900 at 30% opacity, about #D6E9AE) wedge rises from the FY 2022 dark segment top to the FY 2023 bar top, with a thin black arrow pointing up-right from the FY 2022 segment to the top of the FY 2023 bar. Baseline is a thin grey horizontal line under the bars. No legend. Highlighted points: the FY 2022 and FY 2023 bars and the arrow. Right panel contains no graphics other than a thin grey horizontal divider line that fades at both ends, centred between the two text groups at about y 48.4%. NVIDIA logo: green eye-like mark with black wordmark 'NVIDIA' at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold black NVIDIA Sans sans-serif, about 4.45% of slide height (cap height about 3.1%). Subtitle and green accents in regular NVIDIA Sans Regular green #76B900. Body text in regular NVIDIA Sans, black #000000, about 2.22% of slide height. Chart labels in regular NVIDIA Sans, about 1.73% of slide height. Section headings 'Leader in AI & HPC' and 'Growth Drivers' in bold NVIDIA Sans, green #76B900, about 2.47% of slide height. Panels have thin 1 px mid grey (#A0A0A0) hairline borders with square corners and no shadows. Accents are bright green #76B900 and dark olive #598B00. No gradients, no shadows, no corner radii.

**Text**

Data Center / Strong growth fueled by AI performance leadership and huge developer ecosystem / Hyperscale Revenue / P100 / V100 / A100 / 3x / Note: Cumulative Revenue 8Q After Launch / 90,000x / 2015 / K80 / 36,000 Mins / (25 Days) / 2017 / V100 / 480 Mins / (8 Hours) / 2021 / A100 / 24 Seconds / MLPerf / CUDA Downloads – 4.5X in 4 Yrs / 37M / # of Developers – 3.5X in 4 Yrs / 3.5M / Accelerated Applications – 5X in 4 Yrs / 3K / Accelerating Adoption with / Every Architecture / #1 in AI Training & / Inference Performance / The Largest Accelerated / Computing Ecosystem / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Three equal boxed panels sit side by side in a row across the middle of the slide, each with a caption centred beneath it. The slide title Data Center is centred at the top with a green subtitle directly below it, and the NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge. No slide number or footer text is present.

**Visuals**

Three panels. Left: a green vertical bar chart titled by its caption Accelerating Adoption with Every Architecture, y-axis labelled Hyperscale Revenue with no printed scale or values; three bars labelled P100 (short, about 20 percent of the plot height), V100 (about 50 percent) and A100 (tallest, about 90 percent); a black vertical double-headed-style arrow at the centre of the plot labelled 3x runs from the top of the V100 bar to the top of the P100 bar with an arrowhead pointing up; a grey baseline runs under the bars; the note Note: Cumulative Revenue 8Q After Launch sits bottom right. Middle: a horizontal green bar chart titled #1 in AI Training & Inference Performance with a grey vertical axis line; three bars: 2015 K80 at 36,000 Mins (25 Days) (longest bar), 2017 V100 at 480 Mins (8 Hours) (about 13 percent of the longest bar), 2021 A100 at 24 Seconds (a sliver, about 1 percent of the longest bar); a black horizontal arrow labelled 90,000x spans above the bars from the axis toward the right; the MLPerf logo (a grey and blue speedometer gauge icon above the grey word MLPerf) sits below the bars. Right: three infographic rows, each a heading over two green circles sitting on a grey horizontal line: CUDA Downloads – 4.5X in 4 Yrs with a small circle (about 1/4 the diameter of the large circle) and a large circle reading 37M; # of Developers – 3.5X in 4 Yrs with a slightly smaller small circle (about 3/4 of the large circle diameter) and a large circle reading 3.5M; Accelerated Applications – 5X in 4 Yrs with a small circle and a large circle reading 3K. The NVIDIA logo (green and black eye mark with the word NVIDIA) sits bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no texture. Titles and body text are black #000000 in NVIDIASans, a clean geometric humanist sans-serif. Data Center is NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.45 percent of slide height, centred. The subtitle is NVIDIASans Regular in NVIDIA green #76B900, about 2.96 percent. Panel captions are NVIDIASans Medium about 2.47 percent, centred, black. Chart labels are NVIDIASans Regular about 1.73 percent in black; the 3x and 90,000x annotations are NVIDIASans Medium about 2.78 percent. Bars, circles and the thin green edge bar are solid #76B900. Panels are rectangles with a thin mid-grey #A6A6A6 outline about 0.75 pt, square corners, no shadow, no gradient. Axis and baseline lines are dark grey #595959 about 1 pt. Note text is NVIDIASans Italic about 1.23 percent in grey #8C8C8C. Circle numbers are white #FFFFFF on the green circles.

### Slide 19

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Text**

Modern AI is a Data Center Scale Computing Workload / Data centers are becoming AI factories: data as input, intelligence as output / AI Training Computational Requirements / All AI Models Excluding Transformers: 8x / 2yrs / Transformer AI Models: 275x / 2yrs / Megatron-Turing / NLG 530B / GPT-3 / Microsoft T-NLG / GPT-2 / Megatron / XLNet / Wav2Vec 2.0 / MoCo ResNet50 / Xception / InceptionV3 / BERT Large / GPT-1 / Resnet / Seq2Seq / Transformer / ResNeXt / VGG-19 / ELMo / DenseNet201 / AlexNet / Training Compute (petaFLOPS) / 10¹⁰ / 10⁹ / 10⁸ / 10⁷ / 10⁶ / 10⁵ / 10⁴ / 10³ / 10² / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / Fueling Giant-Scale AI Infrastructure / NVIDIA compute & networking GPU | DPU | CPU / Large Language Models, based on the Transformer architecture, are one of / today’s most important advanced AI technologies, involving up to trillions of / parameters that learn from text. / Developing them is an expensive, time-consuming process that demands deep / technical expertise, distributed data center-scale infrastructure, and a full-stack / accelerated computing approach.

**Layout**

Centred title block at the top with a green subtitle beneath it, then two equal boxed columns side by side: a line chart on the left and a photograph with a text panel below it on the right. Both boxes have the same top edge and height, with margins of about 4.5% left and right. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right beneath the right column, with a green vertical bar at the far right edge. There is no slide number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Left: a scatter chart titled 'AI Training Computational Requirements' with a log y-axis labelled 'Training Compute (petaFLOPS)' running from 10² to 10¹⁰ (one decade per gridline) and a linear x-axis from 2012 to 2022 in yearly ticks. Two series: blue points (#0071C5) for non-Transformer models, labelled AlexNet about 6×10² in 2012, Seq2Seq and Resnet about 1.5×10⁴ in 2014 to 2015, VGG-19 about 1.5×10⁴ in 2015, InceptionV3 about 1.5×10⁵ in 2016, Xception about 7×10⁵ in 2016.5, ResNeXt about 2×10⁴ in 2016.7, DenseNet201 about 4×10³ in 2016.8, ELMo about 5×10³ in 2018, Wav2Vec 2.0 about 3×10⁶ in 2019.9, MoCo ResNet50 about 8×10⁵ in 2021, each blue point with its labelled value estimated where none is printed. A straight blue trend line runs from about 2013.9 at 8×10² up to about 2020 at 3×10⁶. Green points (#76B900) for Transformer models, labelled Transformer about 3.5×10⁴ in 2017.6, GPT-1 about 9×10⁴ in 2018.2, BERT Large about 4×10⁵ in 2018.5, XLNet about 3×10⁶ in 2019.1, Megatron about 1.5×10⁷ in 2019.4, MoCo-like point about 1.5×10⁶ in 2019.5, GPT-2 about 3×10⁷ in 2019, Microsoft T-NLG about 5×10⁷ in 2020, GPT-3 about 5×10⁸ in 2020.1, Megatron-Turing NLG 530B about 3×10⁹ in 2021.6, all values estimated. A green straight trend line runs from about 2018.3 at 4×10⁴ up to about 2021.6 at 3×10⁹. Two-line key at top-left of plot: blue 'All AI Models Excluding Transformers: 8x / 2yrs' and green 'Transformer AI Models: 275x / 2yrs'. No legend box; no highlighted points. Chart is on a white plot area with no gridlines, thin grey x-axis line at the bottom. Right: a full-width photograph of a dark data-centre hall, boxed in the upper part of the right panel and not cropped. Subject: rows of black server racks along the back wall with beige-and-black GPU compute trays and networking switch units on a glossy black floor, with a few smaller boards in the foreground. Composition: wide, one-point perspective, horizon near the middle, subjects centred. Lighting: soft studio spotlight from above, dark ambient, highlights on the trays. Dominant colours: near-black #151617, warm tan #8C7B5E and grey. A dark overlay of #151617 at 61% opacity covers the image, with the white heading and subheading placed on top at the upper centre. Bottom-right: NVIDIA logo, black wordmark with green eye symbol (#76B900).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title in NVIDIA Sans Bold, black #000000, about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Subtitle in NVIDIA Sans Regular, green #76B900, about 2.96%, centred. Panel headings in NVIDIA Sans Medium, black #000000 at about 2.47%. Chart key lines and data labels in NVIDIA Sans Regular about 1.48%, blue #0071C5 and green #76B900. Axis tick labels in black at about 1.48%. Body text in NVIDIA Sans Regular, black #000000, about 1.98%, left-aligned, line spacing about 1.1. Photo heading in white NVIDIA Sans Medium, subheading white NVIDIA Sans Regular. Both panels have a 1 px thin grey (#999999) border with square corners and no shadow. Green accent #76B900 used for subtitle, chart series and the right-edge bar. No gradients.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a dense wall of small legal text, many panels on slides 8, 11, 14 and 20 use very small text and low-contrast detail, and several titles are topic labels rather than takeaways.
- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the slide 2 disclaimer and enlarge the tiny text and footnotes on slides 8, 11, 13, 14 and 20, and fix the overlapping data labels on slide 13.
- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Layout**

Three equal-width cards in a row spanning the slide width, separated by even gutters, with about 4.5% side margins and the cards occupying the middle band from about 23.6% to 82% of the slide height. A centred title sits at the top (about 7% from the top) with a centred green subtitle beneath it; each card has its product name centred at its top, an image centred below, a centred bullet list, and a centred period label (for example 2H FY23) beneath the card. The NVIDIA logo sits small at the bottom right with a thin green bar at the far right edge; no slide number or footer text is shown.

**Visuals**

Three product photographs and one logo. (1) H100 GPU: a top-down, square-on render of a black-framed GPU package with a central die showing green, yellow-green and gold tinted circuit blocks and dark heat-spreader frame at the corners; boxed on a flat light grey card with no overlay, centred in its card, fully visible and not cropped. (2) Bluefield-3 DPU: a slightly angled render of a rectangular chip die with a rainbow gradient of gold, orange, green, teal, blue and magenta circuit blocks on a black edge, fully visible, centred in its card. (3) Grace CPU Superchip: a front-on render of a long black module board with two rectangular CPU dies showing small multicolour glowing cores, a black heatsink-like border with copper-coloured mounting dots and a small NVIDIA mark on the left edge; fully visible, centred in its card. (4) NVIDIA logo: small green-and-black wordmark at the bottom right, no boxing. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Cards are flat light grey #F2F2F2 rectangles with a thin mid-grey border of about #A6A6A6 at 0.75 pt and square corners, no shadows. Title is bold black #000000 in NVIDIA Sans Bold, about 4.45% of slide height. Subtitle is regular NVIDIA green #76B900 in NVIDIA Sans Regular, about 2.96% of height. Card titles and period labels are NVIDIA Sans Medium black #000000 at about 2.47% of height. Bullet lines are NVIDIA Sans Regular black #000000 at about 1.98% of height, centred, with roughly 4.0% of height between lines. Sans-serif throughout, no italics. A green accent bar #76B900 sits at the right edge at the bottom.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Text**

Gaming / GeForce — the world’s largest gaming platform / Revenue ($M) / 25% 5-YR CAGR / $12,462 / $7,759 / $6,246 / $5,513 / $5,518 / $5,662 / 1H / 1H / FY 2018 / FY 2019 / FY 2020 / FY 2021 / FY 2022 / FY 2023 / Leader in PC Gaming / Strong #1 market position with over 80% share / 15 of the Top 15 most popular GPUs on Steam / Leading performance & innovation / 200M+ gamers on GeForce / Growth Drivers / Rising adoption of NVIDIA RTX / Expanding universe of gamers & creators / Gaming laptops & game consoles / GeForce NOW Cloud gaming

**Layout**

Two equal boxed panels sit side by side below a centred title block: the title Gaming and green subtitle span the top centre. The left panel (chart) spans roughly x 4.5 to 49.1 percent and the right panel (text) spans x 50.9 to 95.5 percent, both from y 23.6 to 82.0 percent, with thin grey outlines and square corners. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about x 93 to 99 percent, y 95 to 98 percent, with a thin green bar at the far right edge; no slide number or footer text is visible.

**Visuals**

Column chart in the left panel. Title: Revenue ($M), centred above the plot. Subtitle label inside plot top-left: 25% 5-YR CAGR. X axis: fiscal years FY 2018, FY 2019, FY 2020, FY 2021, FY 2022, FY 2023 (categorical, no units); thin dark grey baseline. Y axis: no axis labels or scale printed; bar heights are about proportional to the printed values. Series, all printed above each bar: FY 2018 $5,513; FY 2019 $6,246; FY 2020 $5,518; FY 2021 $7,759; FY 2022 $12,462, a stacked bar whose lower segment (about the bottom 40 percent of the bar, labelled 1H) is dark green and whose upper segment is bright green; FY 2023 $5,662, a bar in dark green labelled 1H. Between FY 2022 and FY 2023 a light green (about 30 percent opacity #76B900 over white) column sits at the 1H level, with a thin black arrow pointing from the FY 2022 1H segment to the FY 2023 bar. No legend. Highlighted points: the FY 2022 total and the 1H segments are the emphasised values. Right panel: four-line centred list of statements, no bullet markers, then a thin grey gradient divider line, then a second centred list. No photographs or icons apart from the NVIDIA logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on white. Title Gaming in black #000000 bold sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans style, about 4.45 percent of slide height). Subtitle in bright green #76B900, regular weight, about 2.96 percent. Chart title and section headers: black #000000 medium for Revenue ($M); green #76B900 bold for Leader in PC Gaming and Growth Drivers (about 2.47 percent). Body and bar labels in black #000000 regular at about 2.22 percent (value labels about 1.73 percent). Bars in #76B900 with darker #598B00 1H segments and a light green 30 percent tint column. Panel outlines in thin mid grey, about #999999, 1 px, square corners, no shadows. Divider is a fading grey horizontal line.

**Layout**

Three equal-width framed panels sit side by side in a row across the middle of the slide, each with a bold centred caption below it, on a white field. The title is centred horizontally near the top, the source line sits bottom-left and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Panel 1 (left): bar chart titled by its caption 'New generation, more gamers'. Vertical green bars (#76B900) with no axis titles, a thin grey horizontal baseline under the bars, category labels Boomers, Gen X, Millennials, Gen Z beneath the baseline, and value labels above each bar: Boomers 42%, Gen X 60%, Millennials 77%, Gen Z 81%. Below the category labels, each bar has a second label: Boomers 2:30 Hrs/Wk, Gen X 4:25 Hrs/Wk, Millennials 6:50 Hrs/Wk, Gen Z 7:20 Hrs/Wk. No legend, no y-axis, no gridlines; bar heights are proportional to the printed percentages on a zero baseline. Panel 2 (centre): two grey-and-green donut charts with white centre holes. Left donut labelled 2017 with centre text 2.3B; green segment PC Gamers is about 27 percent of the ring, starting at 12 o'clock and running clockwise to about 3:30, grey segment Total Gamers is the remainder. Right donut labelled 2021 with centre text 3.1B; green segment is about 25 percent of the ring, from 12 o'clock to 3 o'clock, grey Total Gamers is the remainder. Legend beneath with a green square PC Gamers and a grey square Total Gamers. No axes, no units printed beyond B. Below, centred text reads Expanding reach to / 110M Creators & Broadcasters. Panel 3 (right): heading 340+ RTX Games and Applications in bold green; a row of four app icons, each a rounded-square or circular logo: Adobe Premiere Pro (dark navy square with Pr), Adobe Photoshop (dark blue square with Ps), Blender (orange and dark blue three-dimensional mark), OBS Studio (black circle with white ring). Under each icon, #1 in bold over Video App, Photo App, 3D App, Broadcast App. Below, a grid of about 32 game and application tiles in 8 columns by 4 rows, each tile a square cover image with small gaps; the visible tiles include Forza Horizon, Pandemic, Doom, Red Dead Redemption 2, Metro, Control, Unity, Streaming-style title in row 1; Premiere Pro, a dark title, OBS, Dying Light 2, Lightroom, Horizon, Darktide in row 2; Subnautica, Maya, Nioh-style title, a yellow tile, Fortnite Chapter 3, God of War, Evil Dead, Blender in row 3; a fantasy title, Icarus, a dark tile with red text, Photoshop, Minecraft Dungeons-style title, Ghostwire, a red-lettered title, and a teal tile in row 4. Tile titles that are not legible are not transcribed. The NVIDIA logo appears bottom-right, a green-and-black wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, black titles and body text, green #76B900 accents for the bar chart, donut segments, the panel 3 heading and the right edge bar. Sans-serif geometric typeface NVIDIASans, bold for title and captions, medium for #1 labels and captions, regular for chart labels and small text. Title is bold black about 4.45 percent of slide height. Captions are medium black about 2.47 percent. Body and chart labels are regular black about 1.73 percent. Small hours labels are regular about 1.48 percent. Source line is italic grey #8C8C8C about 1.23 percent. Panels have 1 pixel thin grey #A0A0A0 square-cornered outlines with no fill and no shadow. Bars and donut segments are flat fills with no outline.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Text**

The Next Generation: Ada Lovelace / A quantum leap for gamers | Paves the way for fully simulated worlds / NVIDIA Architecture / 90 Shader / 200 RT / 300 OFA / 1,400 Tensor / 40 Shader / 78 RT / 126 OFA / 320 Tensor / 16 Shader / 49 RT / 130 Tensor / TOPs / Turing / Ampere / Ada / A Giant Leap in Processing / Throughput and Energy / Efficiency – up to 4x faster, / and over 2x the performance / at the same power / Shipping in October / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Centered title and green subtitle across the top of the slide. Below, two boxes sit side by side in the middle band: a bordered bar chart on the left (about x 9 to 49%) and a black photo panel on the right (about x 51 to 95%, y 24 to 82%). A small NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about x 93 to 99%, y 95 to 98%, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge near the bottom.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'NVIDIA Architecture' with y-axis label 'TOPs' (no axis numbers printed, no range shown). Three stacked bars on a baseline: Turing (dark grey #5E5E5E segments, smallest bar, about 0 to 0.1 of height), Ampere (light grey #9A9A9A segments, about 0.35 of the Ada height), Ada (solid green #76B900, tallest). Ada segments labelled from top: 90 Shader, 200 RT, 300 OFA, 1,400 Tensor; Ampere segments labelled from top: 40 Shader, 78 RT, 126 OFA, 320 Tensor; Turing segments labelled from top: 16 Shader, 49 RT, 130 Tensor. No legend; no highlighted points beyond the green Ada bar. Photograph on right: three to four NVIDIA GeForce RTX graphics cards arranged in a diagonal row with the largest card in front labelled RTX 4090 and the others labelled RTX 4080 and RTX 3080 (smaller card at left, partly hidden). Cards are black with silver-grey trim and fans, shown on a black reflective floor with thin green grid lines receding to the back. Lighting is a soft spotlight from above, dominant colours black, silver and green. Full-bleed within a boxed rectangle, not cropped by a frame, with black background behind the text. Text overlaid on the left of the photo in white with green highlight.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, sans-serif, about 4.5% of slide height, centered. Subtitle green #76B900 NVIDIASans Regular, about 3% of slide height, centered. Chart text black #000000 and grey labels #9A9A9A and #5E5E5E in NVIDIASans Medium, about 1.5 to 2% of slide height. Photo caption text white #FFFFFF NVIDIASans Regular about 2.2% of slide height with green #76B900 highlights. Chart box has thin grey (#9A9A9A) 1px outline, square corners, no shadow. Chart bars are flat fills with thin white horizontal separator lines between segments. Photo panel has no border, square corners. A thin green #76B900 vertical accent bar sits at the far right edge near the bottom.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would rewrite topic titles such as 'Gaming' and 'Data Center' as takeaway statements and reduce the near-identical chart-plus-bullets layout that repeats across slides 17 to 25.

**Text**

Professional Visualization / Workstation graphics / Revenue ($M) / 20% 5-YR CAGR / $2,111 / $1,212 / $1,130 / $1,118 / $1,053 / $934 / 1H / 1H / FY 2018 / FY 2019 / FY 2020 / FY 2021 / FY 2022 / FY 2023 / Leader in Workstation Graphics / 90%+ market share in graphics / for workstations / 45M Designers and Creators / Strong software ecosystem with over 70 / supported applications / Growth Drivers / Ray Tracing and AI revolutionizing design / Expanding universe of designers and creators / Collaborative 3D design / Omniverse / Hybrid work environments

**Layout**

Two equal-width boxed panels sit side by side in the lower two-thirds of the slide: a left chart panel (about 4.5% to 49.2% of width) and a right text panel (about 50.9% to 95.5%), both spanning about 23.6% to 82.0% of height with thin grey outlines. The centred title and green subtitle sit at the top centre, the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right, and a thin green vertical bar sits at the far right edge, with no page number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled 'Revenue ($M)' with the note '20% 5-YR CAGR' at its upper left. Categories along the x-axis: FY 2018, FY 2019, FY 2020, FY 2021, FY 2022, FY 2023. Values printed above each bar: FY 2018 $934; FY 2019 $1,130; FY 2020 $1,212; FY 2021 $1,053; FY 2022 $2,111; FY 2023 $1,118. No y-axis, gridlines or axis units are shown beyond the ($M) title; bar heights are proportional to the printed values. FY 2022 is a stacked bar: a lower dark green segment labelled 1H (about $1,000M of the total, estimated from its height) and an upper bright green segment. FY 2023 is a dark green bar labelled 1H, with a lighter translucent green wedge (about 30% opacity #76B900 over white, showing as #D6EAB3) rising from the FY 2022 1H top to the FY 2023 bar top with a thin black arrow pointing to the FY 2023 bar. The horizontal baseline is a thin grey line under the bars. Right panel contains no chart; a short horizontal gradient divider line (dark grey at centre, fading to white at both ends) separates the two text groups.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no texture. Titles and accents use NVIDIA green #76B900; the main title and body text are black #000000. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif (NVIDIASans family); the title is Bold at about 4.45% of slide height, the subtitle Regular at about 2.96%, panel headings Bold at about 2.47%, body and labels Regular at about 2.22% and chart value labels at about 1.73%. Bar fills use #76B900 and dark shade #598B00. Panel outlines are thin mid-grey rectangles with square corners and no shadows. The 1H labels are white on the dark green segments.

**Layout**

Two equal bordered panels sit side by side in the content area: a left panel (x 4.5 to 49.2, y 23.6 to 82.0) holding a bar chart and three count boxes, and a right panel (x 50.9 to 95.5, same top and bottom) holding centred text, a divider and a Growth Drivers block. The title is centred at the top (y 7.3) with a green subtitle centred beneath it; the footnote is right-aligned under the right panel and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a thin green bar at the far right edge. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Revenue ($M)', columns labelled FY 2018 to FY 2023 on the category axis. Values printed above bars: FY 2018 $558, FY 2019 $641, FY 2020 $700, FY 2021 $536, FY 2022 $566 (split into a dark-green 1H lower segment and a lighter green upper segment), FY 2023 $358 (dark-green 1H block with a pale-green connector block of about 1H from FY 2022 to FY 2023 and a thin black arrow rising from the FY 2022 1H segment to the FY 2023 bar top). Value axis is hidden; invisible white tick labels 0, 200, 400, 600, 800, 1,000, 1,200 and 1,400 sit at the left of the axis (white on white, not visible), so the implied axis range is about 0 to 1,400 $M. Bars are solid #76B900 with a darker #5A8A00 for the 1H segments and a pale #D5E8A8 connector block. Horizontal baseline is grey #999999, about 0.2% of slide height thick. Three count boxes with thin grey #999999 outlines: '20 of 30 Passenger EV', '7 of 10 Trucking', '8 of 10 Robotaxi'; numerals in green bold. No logos or photographs other than the NVIDIA logo bottom right. No legend and no highlighted points beyond the arrow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles are black NVIDIA Sans Bold, the main title about 4.45% of slide height; the subtitle and section headings use green #76B900 (Regular for the subtitle, Bold for headings). Body text is black NVIDIA Sans Regular at about 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900. Panels have square corners with thin mid-grey #999999 borders, no shadow. The divider in the right panel is a horizontal line fading from dark grey #666666 at the centre to transparent at both ends. Footnote is NVIDIA Sans Italic in grey #8C8C8C at about 1.24% of slide height.

**Layout**

A single centred column on a plain white slide. The title Summary sits centred near the top at about 7% from the top edge, and six centred text lines are stacked in the middle of the slide at even vertical intervals of about 7.7% of slide height, starting at about 33% from the top. The NVIDIA logo sits in the bottom-right corner, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge beside it. There is no footer text, slide number or subtitle.

**Visuals**

The slide has no photographs, charts, tables or diagrams. The only non-text visual is the NVIDIA logo in the bottom-right corner: the green eye-shaped mark followed by the black NVIDIA wordmark, about 5.5% of slide width. A thin solid green vertical bar sits at the right edge of the slide next to the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, pattern or texture. Title is black #000000 NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.45% of slide height, centred. Body lines are NVIDIA green #76B900 NVIDIASans Regular, about 3.33% of slide height, centred, with no bullets, no underlines and no shadows. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900 for the body text, the logo eye mark and the edge bar. Text is sans-serif throughout. No shapes, rounded corners or borders apart from the edge bar.

**Text**
