GAAP Financial Measures

**Layout**

Single left-aligned title block set in the left half, starting about 26% down the slide and running to about 38%, with a 4.3% left margin. A thin vertical green bar sits flush on the left edge from the top to about 36.5% of the height. No footer, page number or other text is present, and the right side is occupied by the photograph.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph covers the whole slide. It shows abstract three-dimensional glowing green ribbon or tube forms curving in from the right and bottom edges, with fine light streaks sweeping diagonally from the lower left toward the upper right. The forms are cropped by the right and bottom edges and are sharpest on the right half. Lighting is emissive, with bright lime-green highlights on the forms against a deep black field. Dominant colours are black #000000, green #76B900 and pale lime highlights. The image is not boxed and has no overlay or tint. A small NVIDIA logo image sits in the bottom-right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of green glowing light forms on top. Pure black #000000 background with a full-bleed dark photograph of green light forms. Title text is white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Bold, a bold sans-serif, at about 5.56% of slide height, set left-aligned with tight leading between the two lines. The only accent is a flat green #76B900 vertical bar on the left edge, with square corners and no shadow or gradient. A second full-slide #76B900 fill is recorded in the file but is covered by the black fill and the photograph.

Stock-based compensation charge was allocated to cost of goods sold, research and development expense, and sales, general and administrative expense / C. Comprises of IP-related costs, legal settlement costs, and contributions

**Layout**

Single full-width table on a white slide. The title is centred horizontally at the top (about y 7 to 12 percent), with the table spanning x 6.9 to 93.1 and y 19.3 to about 88 to 91 percent. A black header row sits at y 19.3 to 33.2 percent, followed by 14 body rows in 7 period pairs, with the period label in the first column spanning each pair. Footnotes A, B and C are left-aligned at x 7.1 near the bottom, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right at x 93.1 to 98.6. No page number is visible.

**Visuals**

Table with 8 columns: Operating Margin ("$ in Millions & Margin Percentage") as the row-label column; Non-GAAP; Acquisition Termination Cost; Acquisition-Related and Other Costs (A); Stock-Based Compensation (B); Other (3); GAAP. Rows are grouped by period, each period having a dollar row ($ in millions) and a margin percentage row: FY 2018 ($3,617 / 37.2% non-GAAP; — / — termination; (13) / (0.2) acquisition-related; (391) / (4.0) stock-based; (3) / — other; $3,210 / 33.0% GAAP). FY 2019 ($4,407 / 37.6%; —; (2) / —; (557) / (4.7); (44) / (0.4); $3,804 / 32.5%). FY 2020 ($3,735 / 34.2%; —; (31) / (0.3); (844) / (7.7); (14) / (0.1); $2,846 / 26.1%). FY 2021 ($6,803 / 40.8%; —; (836) / (5.0); (1,397) / (8.4); (38) / (0.2); $4,532 / 27.2%). FY 2022 ($12,690 / 47.2%; —; (636) / (2.5); (2,004) / (7.4); (9) / —; $10,041 / 37.3%). 1H FY22 ($5,628 / 46.3%; —; (325) / (2.7); (894) / (7.3); (9) / (0.1); $4,400 / 36.2%). 1H FY23 ($5,280 / 35.2%; acquisition termination cost (1,353) / (9.0); (324) / (2.2); (1,227) / (8.2); (9) / —; $2,367 / 15.8%). Dashes mark nil values. Table has no chart, no photograph and no highlighted points. Cell rules are thin mid-grey horizontal lines between rows and vertical lines between columns. The only logo is the NVIDIA wordmark with a green eye symbol, bottom right. Header fill black, header text white.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or texture. Title in heavy geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold style, close to Helvetica Bold) in black #000000. Table header row is solid black #000000 with white #FFFFFF medium-weight sans text. Body text is regular-weight black #000000 sans-serif. Footnotes are italic sans in grey #8C8C8C. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 in the logo and in a thin vertical bar at the right edge. Title is about 4.5 percent of slide height, body cells about 2.2 percent, header labels about 2.5 percent, footnotes about 1.2 percent. Table rules are thin grey #A6A6A6 lines, 1 px, with square corners and no shadows.

### Slide 31

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covering the entire slide with no title, body text, footer or slide number. The NVIDIA logo sits in the upper-left area, left-aligned at roughly 2 percent from the left edge and about 13 percent from the top.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed abstract 3D render: a glossy, metallic green and black NVIDIA eye-shaped emblem seen in perspective, with concentric layered bands and a split down the middle, sweeping from the centre-right toward the upper right. Thin green and grey-white light streaks flow in from the left and lower edge. Lighting is a directional green rim light on the bands against a near-black field. Dominant colours are black #000000 and green #76B900 with grey-white highlights. The image is not boxed and has no overlay or tint. The NVIDIA logo (green eye mark with white NVIDIA wordmark and registered mark) sits at upper left. A second small image sits at the bottom right, but no visible content is discernible against the black background.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000, overlaid by the full-bleed abstract green and black 3D photograph. Black background with the full-bleed render as the only visual field. The NVIDIA logo uses green #76B900 for the eye mark and white #FFFFFF for the wordmark. No titles, body text or typefaces are present. The render has glossy highlights and layered translucent streaks, with no shapes, corner radii or shadows.
